# Skills · I · part 15

838 entries, inbox-guardian-for-gmail to incident-triage-lite. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **inbox-guardian-for-gmail**: Review a personal Gmail inbox with local, owner-approved spam rules. Use for audit-first quarantine, Trash, repeated-evidence sender learning, and header review.
